     ConfigSource::Default => write!(f, \"built-in default\"),\n1487\t            ConfigSource::GlobalFile(p) => write!(f, \"{}\", p.display()),\n1488\t            ConfigSource::WorkspaceFile(p) =>      }\n1492\t    }\n1493\t}\n1494\t\n1495\t/// Map of config field paths to their source.\n1496\t///\n1497\t/// Used by `needle config --dump --show-source` to annotate each value.\n1498\tpub type Only these sections are allowed at the workspace level:\n1507\t/// - `agent.default`, `agent.timeout`\n1508\t/// - `strands` (weave, pulse, unravel)\n1509\t/// - `prompt.*`\n1510\t/// - `verification` Clone, Default, Deserialize)]\n1515\tpub struct WorkspaceOverrides {\n1516\t    #[serde(default)]\n1517\t    pub agent: Option<WorkspaceAgentOverrides>,\n1518\t    #[serde(default)]\n1519\t    pub strands: Option<WorkspaceStrandsOverrides>,\n1520\t    #[serde(default)]\n1521\t    pub prompt: Option<PromptConfig>,\n1522\t    /// Verification commands run after agent success, before accepting closure.\n1523\t    /// Legacy format — prefer `gates` for new configurations.\n1524\t    #[serde(default)]\n1525\t    pub verification: Option<Vec<String>>,\n1526\t    /// Pluggable validation gates.\n1527\t    #[serde(default)]\n1528\t    pub gates: Option<Vec<GateConfig>>,\n1529\t    /// Workspace identity labels (overridable per-workspace).\n1530\t    ///\n1531\t    /// Set in `.needle.yaml` under `workspace.labels`. ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n1564\t// Top-level Config\n1565\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n1566\t\n1567\t/// Fully resolved NEEDLE configuration.\n1568\t///\n1569\t/// Loaded once at boot, immutable during a !path.exists() {\n1663\t            return Ok(Config::default());\n1664\t        }\n1665\t        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)\n1666\t            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read ignored\",\n1705\t                            key_str,\n1706\t                        );\n1707\t                    }\n1708\t                }\n1709\t            }\n1710\t        }\n1711\t        Ok(())\n1712\t    }\n1713\t\n1714\t    /// Apply workspace overrides to a config.\n1715\t    ///\n1716\t    /// Only overridable fields are applied. }\n1933\t\n1934\t    /// Apply CLI overrides with source tracking.\n1935\t    pub fn apply_cli_overrides(\n1936\t        config: &mut Config,\n1937\t        overrides: CliOverrides,\n1938\t        sources: &mut SourceMap,\n1939\t    ) {\n1940\t        if let Some(ws) = overrides.workspace {\n1941\t            config.workspace.default = ws;\n1942\t level, not stored in Config\n1953\t    }\n1954\t\n1955\t    /// Load the fully resolved configuration using the complete hierarchy:\n1956\t    ///\n1957\t    /// defaults → global file → workspace `.needle.yaml` → env vars → CLI args\n1958\t    ///\n1959\t    /// Returns the resolved config and a source map showing where each value\n1960\t    /// came from. The source map only contains entries for values that were\n1961\t    /// overridden from their defaults.\n1962\t    pub fn load_resolved(workspace_root: &Path, cli: CliOverrides) -> Result<(Config, SourceMap)> {\n1963\t        let mut sources = SourceMap::new();\n1964\t\n1965\t        // Layer 1 + 2: defaults + global config.\n1966\t        let global_path = dirs_or_home(\".config/needle/config.yaml\");\n1967\t        let mut config = Self::load_from_path(&global_path)?;\n1968\t\n1969\t        // Track which fields came from global config (if file existed).\n1970\t        if global_path.exists() {\n1971\t            let source = ConfigSource::GlobalFile(global_path);\n1972\t            // Mark all top-level sections as from global.\n1973\t            for key in &[\n1974\t                \"agent.default\",\n1975\t                \"agent.timeout\",\n1976\t                \"worker.max_workers\",\n1977\t                \"worker.idle_timeout\",\n1978\t                \"health.heartbeat_interval_secs\",\n1979\t                \"health.heartbeat_ttl_secs\",\n1980\t            ] {\n1981\t                sources.insert((*key).to_string(), source.clone());\n1982\t            }\n1983\t        }\n1984\t\n1985\t        // Layer 3: workspace config.\n1986\t        if let Some(ws_overrides) = Self::load_workspace(workspace_root)? {\n1987\t            Self::apply_workspace(&mut config, &ws_overrides, workspace_root, &mut sources);\n1988\t        }\n1989\t\n1990\t        // Layer 4: environment variables.\n1991\t        Self::apply_env_overrides(&mut config, &mut sources);\n1992\t\n1993\t        // Layer 5: CLI arguments.\n1994\t        Self::apply_cli_overrides(&mut config, cli, &mut sources);\n1995\t\n1996\t        Ok((config, sources))\n1997\t    }\n1998\t\n1999\t    /// Validate a resolved config.\n2000\t    ///\n2001\t    /// Returns a list of errors (empty = valid).\n2002\t    pub fn validate(config: &Config) -> Vec<ConfigError> {\n2003\t        let mut errors = Vec::new();\n2004\t\n2005\t        if config.agent.default.is_empty() {\n2006\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2007\t                field: \"agent.default\".to_string(),\n2008\t                message: \"must not be empty\".to_string(),\n2009\t            });\n2010\t        }\n2011\t\n2012\t        if config.worker.max_workers == 0 {\n2013\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2014\t                field: \"worker.max_workers\".to_string(),\n2015\t                message: \"must be at least 1\".to_string(),\n2016\t            });\n2017\t        }\n2018\t\n2019\t        if config.worker.max_workers > 50 {\n2020\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2021\t                field: \"worker.max_workers\".to_string(),\n2022\t                message: format!(\n2023\t                    \"{} exceeds practical fleet limit of 50\",\n2024\t                    config.worker.max_workers\n2025\t                ),\n2026\t            });\n2027\t        }\n2028\t\n2029\t        if config.worker.cpu_load_warn <= 0.0 || config.worker.cpu_load_warn > 1.0 {\n2030\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2031\t                field: \"worker.cpu_load_warn\".to_string(),\n2032\t                message: \"must be in range (0.0, 1.0]\".to_string(),\n2033\t         }\n254\t        }\n255\t    }\n256\t\n257\t    /// Path to this worker's heartbeat file.\n258\t    ///\n259\t    /// Keyed by fully-qualified identity (`{adapter}-{worker_id}`) to      }\n339\t        }\n340\t\n341\t        Ok(heartbeats)\n342\t    }\n343\t\n344\t    /// Check whether a heartbeat is stale (exceeded TTL).\n345\t    pub fn is_stale(heartbeat: &HeartbeatData, ttl: .unwrap_or(Duration::ZERO);\n350\t        age > ttl\n351\t    }\n352\t\n353\t    /// Check whether a process with the given PID is alive.\n354\t    ///\n355\t    /// Uses `kill(pid, 0)` semantics: Returns true if the process\n357\t    /// exists (including EPERM — process exists but we can't signal it).\n358\t    pub fn check_pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {\n359\t        if pid == 0 {\n360\t            return false;\n361\t        }\n362\t        // SAFETY: kill(pid, 0) only checks existence; no signal is delivered.\n363\t        let rc = unsafe { libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t, 0) };\n364\t        if rc == 0 {\n365\t            return true;\n366\t        }\n367\t        // ESRCH means no such process. EPERM means the process exists but we\n368\t        // lack permission to send signals to it (process is alive).\n369\t        let errno = unsafe { *libc::__errno_location() };\n370\t        errno == libc::EPERM\n371\t    }\n372\t\n373\t    /// Detect peers with stale heartbeats.\n374\t    ///\n375\t    /// Returns a list of stale peers, excluding this worker.\n376\t    pub fn      current_task,\n443\t            model,\n444\t            heartbeat_file: None,\n445\t        };\n446\t\n447\t        let path = self.heartbeat_path();\n448\t        let tmp_path =      if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {\n453\t            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)\n454\t                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to create heartbeat dir: {}\", ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n484\t// StalePeer\n485\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n486\t\n487\t/// A peer worker detected as having a stale heartbeat.\n488\t#[derive(Debug)]\n489\tpub struct StalePeer {\n490\t    pub worker_id: String,\n491\t    /// Fully-qualified identity of the peer.\n492\t    pub qualified_id: Option<String>,\n493\t    pub pid: u32,\n494\t    pub pid_alive: bool,\n495\t    pub current_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n496\t    pub last_heartbeat: DateTime<Utc>,\n497\t    pub heartbeat_file: PathBuf,\n498\t}\n499\t\n500\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n501\t// Emitter loop (runs in a dedicated std::thread)\n502\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n503\t\n504\t/// Maximum sleep between heartbeat attempts when backing off on failure.\n505\tconst MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5 * 60);\n506\t\n507\t/// Sleep interval for interruptible sleep — check shutdown flag every 100ms.\n508\t///\n509\t/// This ensures the heartbeat emitter responds to shutdown signals within 100ms\n510\t/// instead of sleeping for the full heartbeat interval (default 60s). When the\n511\t/// worker is killed (e.g., by the capacity governor), this gives the atexit\n512\t/// handler a better chance to emit worker.stopped telemetry.\n513\tconst INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);\n514\t\n515\t/// Background emitter loop. Writes heartbeat at each interval.\n516\t///\n517\t/// Circuit breaker: after `max_consecutive_failures` consecutive write failures\n518\t/// the loop sets the shutdown flag and exits so the worker terminates instead of\n519\t/// spinning indefinitely.\n520\t///\n521\t/// Backoff: each consecutive failure doubles the inter-attempt sleep, capped at\n522\t/// [`MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF`].\n523\t///\n524\t/// Uses an interruptible sleep pattern to respond quickly to shutdown signals.\n525\t#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n526\tfn emitter_loop(\n527\t    shared_state: Arc<Mutex<SharedHeartbeatState>>,\n528\t    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n529\t    heartbeat_dir: PathBuf,\n530\t    worker_id: String,\n531\t    qualified_id: String,\n532\t    workspace: PathBuf,\n533\t    started_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n534\t    interval: Duration,\n535\t    max_consecutive_failures: u32,\n536\t) {\n537\t    // Ensure the heartbeat directory exists before entering the write loop so\n538\t    // that workers self-recover if ~/.needle/state/heartbeats/ is deleted.\n539\t    if let Err(e) = std::fs::create_dir_all(&heartbeat_dir) {\n540\t        tracing::error!(\n541\t            error = %e,\n542\t            dir = %heartbeat_dir.display(),\n543\t            \"failed to create heartbeat directory\"\n544\t        );\n545\t    }\n546\t\n547\t    let mut consecutive_failures: u32 = 0;\n548\t    let mut current_sleep = interval;\n549\t    let mut elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n550\t\n551\t    loop {\n552\t        // Interruptible sleep: check shutdown flag every 100ms instead of\n553\t        // sleeping for the full interval. This ensures the emitter responds\n554\t        // to shutdown signals quickly, giving the atexit handler a chance to\n555\t        // emit worker.stopped telemetry even if the process is killed.\n556\t        while elapsed < current_sleep {\n557\t            if shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {\n558\t                tracing::debug!(worker = %worker_id, \"heartbeat emitter shutting down\");\n559\t                return;\n560\t            }\n561\t            let sleep_dur = std::cmp::min(INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL, current_sleep - elapsed);\n562\t            std::thread::sleep(sleep_dur);\n563\t            elapsed += sleep_dur;\n564\t        }\n565\t        elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n566\t\n567\t        let (state, current_bead, beads_processed, current_workspace, model) =\n568\t            match shared_state.lock() {\n569\t                Ok(guard) => (\n570\t                    guard.state.clone(),\n571\t                    guard.current_bead.clone(),\n572\t                    guard.beads_processed,\n573\t                    guard.current_workspace.clone(),\n574\t                    guard.model.clone(),\n575\t                ),\n576\t                Err(_) => {\n577\t                    // Mutex poisoned — the main thread panicked. Exit.\n578\t                    tracing::error!(\n579\t                        worker = %worker_id,\n580\t                        \"shared state mutex poisoned, heartbeat emitter exiting\"\n581\t                    );\n582\t                    return;\n583\t                }\n584\t            };\n585\t\n586\t        // Use the current bead's workspace if set, otherwise fall back to home workspace.\n587\t        let effective_workspace = current_workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| workspace.clone());\n588\t\n589\t        let is_idle = state == WorkerState::Exhausted || current_bead.is_none();\n590\t        let current_task = current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.to_string());\n591\t\n592\t        let data = HeartbeatData {\n593\t            worker_id: worker_id.clone(),\n594\t            qualified_id: qualified_id.clone(),\n595\t            pid: std::process::id(),\n596\t            state,\n597\t            current_bead,\n598\t            workspace: effective_workspace,\n599\t            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n600\t            started_at,\n601\t            beads_processed,\n602\t            session: worker_id.clone(),\n603\t            is_idle,\n604\t            current_task,\n605\t            model,\n606\t            heartbeat_file: None,\n607\t        };\n608\t\n609\t        let path = heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", qualified_id));\n610\t        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n611\t\n612\t        let write_result: anyhow::Result<()> = (|| {\n613\t            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data)?;\n614\t            std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json.as_bytes())?;\n615\t            std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &path)?;\n616\t            Ok(())\n617\t        })();\n618\t\n619\t        match write_result {\n620\t            Ok(()) => {\n621\t                consecutive_failures = 0;\n622\t                current_sleep = interval;\n623\t            }\n624\t            Err(e) => {\n625\t                consecutive_failures += 1;\n626\t                tracing::error!(\n627\t                    error = %e,\n628\t                    worker = %worker_id,\n629\t                    consecutive_failures,\n630\t                    max = max_consecutive_failures,\n631\t                    \"heartbeat write failed\"\n632\t                );\n633\t                if consecutive_failures >= max_consecutive_failures {\n634\t                    tracing::error!(\n635\t                        worker = %worker_id,\n636\t                        consecutive_failures,\n637\t                        \"heartbeat emitter circuit breaker triggered — worker will shut down\"\n638\t                    );\n639\t                    // Emit a final heartbeat event before shutting down so the\n640\t                    // telemetry log shows the circuit breaker was the cause.\n641\t                    let _ = std::fs::write(\n642\t                        heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}-circuit-breaker.txt\", qualified_id)),\n643\t                        format!(\n644\t                            \"Circuit breaker tripped after {} consecutive heartbeat write failures\\n\\\n645\t                             Worker: {}\\n\\\n646\t                             Qualified ID: {}\\n\\\n647\t                             Last error: {}\\n\\\n648\t                             Timestamp: {}\",\n649\t                            consecutive_failures,\n650\t                            worker_id,\n651\t                            qualified_id,\n652\t                            e,\n653\t                            Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n654\t                        ),\n655\t                    );\n656\t                    shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n657\t                    return;\n658\t                }\n659\t                // Exponential backoff to reduce log spam before the circuit breaker fires.\n660\t                current_sleep = current_sleep.saturating_mul(2).min(MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF);\n661\t            }\n662\t        }\n663\t    }\n664\t}\n665\t\n666\t// .unwrap()\n680\t            .parent()\n681\t            .unwrap()\n682\t            .to_path_buf();\n683\t        config.health.heartbeat_interval_secs = 1;\n684\t /// Verify that the circuit breaker fires after N consecutive write failures:\n976\t    /// the emitter must set the shutdown flag and return rather than looping forever.\n977\t    #[test]\n978\t    fn emitter_exits_after_consecutive_failures() {\n979\t        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;\n980\t\n981\t        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n982\t        let hb_dir = dir.path().join(\"state\").join(\"heartbeats\");\n983\t        std::fs::create_dir_all(&hb_dir).unwrap();\n984\t\n985\t        // Make the heartbeat directory read-only so every write attempt fails.\n986\t        std::fs::set_permissions(&hb_dir, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o555)).unwrap();\n987\t\n988\t        // Verify the directory is actually unwritable. When running as root\n989\t        // (e.g., in CI containers), 0o555 doesn't block writes and the emitter\n990\t        // loop would never hit its failure threshold, hanging the test forever.\n991\t        let probe = hb_dir.join(\".write-probe\");\n992\t        let unwritable = std::fs::write(&probe, b\"x\").is_err();\n993\t        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&probe);\n994\t        if !unwritable {\n995\t            // Root bypasses permission checks — skip this test.\n996\t            let _ = std::fs::set_permissions(&hb_dir, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755));\n997\t            return;\n998\t        }\n999\t\n1000\t        let shared_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(SharedHeartbeatState {\n1001\t            state: WorkerState::Selecting,\n1002\t            current_bead: None,\n1003\t            beads_processed: 0,\n1004\t            current_workspace: None,\n1005\t            model: \"claude-sonnet-4\".to_string(),\n1006\t        }));\n1007\t        let shutdown = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));\n1008\t\n1009\t        let shutdown_clone = shutdown.clone();\n1010\t        let shared_state_clone = shared_state.clone();\n1011\t        let hb_dir_clone = hb_dir.clone();\n1012\t\n1013\t        // Use a tiny interval and a low failure threshold so the test completes quickly.\n1014\t        let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || {\n1015\t            emitter_loop(\n1016\t                shared_state_clone,\n1017\t                shutdown_clone,\n1018\t                hb_dir_clone,\n1019\t                \"cb-test\".to_string(),\n1020\t                \"claude-cb-test\".to_string(),\n1021\t                PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"),\n1022\t                Utc::now(),\n1023\t                Duration::from_millis(1),\n1024\t                3, // trip after 3 consecutive failures\n1025\t            );\n1026\t        });\n1027\t\n1028\t        handle.join().expect(\"emitter thread panicked\");\n1029\t\n1030\t        // The circuit breaker must have handler.\n70\t#[cfg(unix)]\n71\tstatic LAST_SIGNAL: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(0);\n72\t\n73\t/// Synchronous signal handler for SIGTERM, SIGINT, and SIGHUP.\n74\t///\n75\t/// This function is called directly by the OS when a signal is received.\n76\t/// It must be async-signal-safe: no allocation, no locking, no I/O.\n77\t/// We set the atomic shutdown flag, record the signal number, and return immediately.\n78\t#[cfg(unix)]\n79\textern \"C\" fn signal_handler(sig: i32) {\n80\t    // SAFETY: The signal handler is only installed after set_global_shutdown_flag\n81\t    // has been called with a valid pointer. The pointer remains valid for the\n82\t    // entire lifetime of the worker process.\n83\t    let ptr = GLOBAL_SHUTDOWN_FLAG.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) as *const AtomicBool;\n84\t    if !ptr.is_null() {\n85\t        // SAFETY: The pointer is valid and points to an AtomicBool that lives\n86\t        // for the entire program duration.\n87\t        unsafe {\n88\t            (*ptr).store(true, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n89\t        }\n90\t        // Record the signal number so the main loop can log it.\n91\t        LAST_SIGNAL.store(sig as u32, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n92\t    }\n93\t}\n94\t\n95\t/// Install synchronous signal handlers for SIGTERM, SIGINT, and SIGHUP.\n96\t///\n97\t/// Uses libc::sigaction to register handlers that set the shutdown flag\n98\t/// immediately when a signal is received. If HANDLING state exceeds the\n495\t    /// timeout, the watchdog sets the `watchdog_triggered` flag, which\n496\t    /// the main worker loop checks to force recovery.\n497\t    fn start_watchdog_thread(&mut self) {\n498\t        let watchdog_triggered = self.watchdog_triggered.clone();\n499\t        let handling_state_entered_at_ptr =\n500\t            &self.handling_state_entered_at as *const Option<Instant> as usize;\n501\t\n502\t        let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || {\n503\t            loop {\n504\t                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5));\n505\t                if watchdog_triggered.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {\n506\t                    // Watchdog has already triggered, exit the thread.\n507\t                    break;\n508\t                }\n509\t                // Check if we've been in HANDLING state for too long.\n510\t                // We read the timestamp from the Worker struct via the pointer.\n511\t                // SAFETY: The Worker struct outlives the watchdog thread because\n512\t                // the thread is joined when the Worker is dropped.\n513\t                let entered_at = unsafe {\n514\t                    let ptr = handling_state_entered_at_ptr as *const Option<Instant>;\n515\t                    (*ptr).as_ref().copied()\n516\t                };\n517\t\n518\t                if let Some(entry_time) = entered_at {\n519\t                    let elapsed = entry_time.elapsed().as_secs();\n520\t                    if elapsed >= HANDLING_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT_SECS {\n521\t                        tracing::error!(\n522\t                            elapsed_secs = elapsed,\n523\t                            \"HANDLING state watchdog triggered - forcing recovery\"\n524\t                        );\n525\t                        watchdog_triggered.store(true, Ordering::Release);\n526\t                        break;\n527\t                    }\n528\t                }\n529\t            }\n530\t        });\n531\t\n532\t        self.watchdog_handle = Some(handle);\n533\t    }\n534\t\n535\t    /// Run the worker loop until exhausted, stopped, or errored.\n536\t    ///\n537\t    /// The main loop is a match on `self.state`. Every state has a handler\n538\t    /// that performs its actions and sets `self.state` to the next state.\n539\t    ///\n540\t    /// Guarantees that the telemetry BufWriter is flushed before returning,\n541\t    /// even when the inner state machine exits early via `?`.\n542\t    pub async fn run(&mut self) -> Result<WorkerState> {\n543\t        // Start the telemetry writer now that we are inside the tokio runtime.\n544\t        self.telemetry.start();\n545\t\n546\t        // IMMEDIATE boot event — must be the first thing emitted after telemetry starts.\n547\t        // This ensures we get a trace even if subsequent init steps block indefinitely.\n548\t        // Skip if already emitted externally (e.g., from CLI layer for early boot diagnostics).\n549\t        if !self.booting_emitted {\n550\t            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerBooting {\n551\t                worker_name: self.worker_name.clone(),\n552\t                version: env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\").to_string(),\n553\t            })?;\n554\t            // Force-flush to disk before boot() — if init blocks, we still have a trace.\n555\t            self.telemetry\n556\t                .force_flush_async(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))\n557\t                .await?;\n558\t        }\n559\t\n560\t        let result = self.run_inner().await;\n561\t\n562\t        // Safety-net flush: shutdown() is idempotent. Normal terminal paths\n563\t        // (stop, handle_exhausted, Errored) already call it; this catches\n564\t        // any early-exit via `?` (boot failure, state handler panic, etc.)\n565\t        // so the BufWriter is always flushed before the tokio Runtime drops.\n566\t        self.telemetry.shutdown().await;\n567\t\n568\t        result\n569\t    }\n570\t\n571\t    /// Inner state machine — called only from [`run()`](Self::run).\n572\t    ///\n573\t    /// May return early via `?` without calling `telemetry.shutdown()`;\n574\t    /// `run()` handles the safety-net flush.\n575\t    async fn run_inner(&mut self) -> Result<WorkerState> {\n576\t        // Boot: validate config and initialize.\n577\t        self.boot()?;\n578\t\n579\t        // Start the watchdog thread that monitors HANDLING state duration.\n580\t        // This must be started after boot() so the Worker struct is fully initialized.\n581\t        self.start_watchdog_thread();\n582\t\n583\t        // Install signal handlers.\n584\t        self.install_signal_handlers();\n585\t\n586\t        // Create the worker.session root span that encompasses the entire worker lifecycle.\n587\t        let worker_id = self.qualified_id();\n588\t        let session_span = tracing::info_span!(\n589\t            \"worker.session\",\n590\t            needle.worker_id = %worker_id,\n591\t            needle.session_id = %self.telemetry.session_id(),\n592\t            needle.agent = %self.config.agent.default,\n593\t            needle.model = %self.config.agent.default, // Will be updated when adapter is resolved\n594\t            needle.workspace = %self.config.workspace.default.display(),\n595\t        );\n596\t        let _enter = session_span.enter();\n597\t\n598\t        loop {\n599\t            // Check for shutdown signal between states.\n600\t            if self.shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {\n601\t                // Retrieve and clear the last received signal for logging.\n602\t                #[cfg(unix)]\n603\t                let signal_name = {\n604\t                    let sig = LAST_SIGNAL.swap(0, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n605\t                    if sig == 0 {\n606\t                        None\n607\t                    } else {\n608\t                        Some(match sig {\n609\t                            1 => \"SIGHUP\",\n610\t                            2 => \"SIGINT\",\n611\t                            15 => \"SIGTERM\",\n612\t                            _ => \"unknown signal\",\n613\t                        })\n614\t                    }\n615\t                };\n616\t                #[cfg(not(unix))]\n617\t                let signal_name = None;\n618\t\n619\t                let reason = if let Some(name) = signal_name {\n620\t                    format!(\"signal received ({name})\")\n621\t                } else {\n622\t                    \"signal received\".to_string()\n623\t                };\n624\t\n625\t                match self.state {\n626\t                    // If we're in the middle of processing a bead, handle it\n627\t                    // as interrupted so the bead gets released.\n628\t                    WorkerState::Building\n629\t                    | WorkerState::Dispatching\n630\t                    | WorkerState::Executing\n631\t                    | WorkerState::Handling => {\n632\t                        // Let the current state handler finish, but mark\n633\t                        // that we should stop after handling.\n634\t                    }\n635\t                    // For states where we don't hold a bead, stop immediately.\n636\t                    WorkerState::Selecting\n637\t                    | WorkerState::Claiming\n638\t                    | WorkerState::Retrying\n639\t                    | WorkerState::Logging => {\n640\t                        // Release any claimed bead before stopping.\n641\t                        if let Some(ref bead) = self.current_bead {\n642\t                            let bead_id = bead.id.clone();\n643\t                            tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead_id, \"releasing bead on shutdown\");\n644\t                            let _ = self.store.release(&bead_id).await;\n645\t                        }\n646\t                        return self.stop(&reason).await;\n647\t                    }\n648\t                    WorkerState::Stopped | WorkerState::Exhausted | WorkerState::Errored => {\n649\t                        return self.stop(&reason).await;\n650\t                    }\n651\t                    WorkerState::Booting => {\n652\t                        return self.stop(\"signal received during boot\").await;\n653\t                    }\n654\t                }\n655\t            }\n656\t\n657\t            // Check for watchdog trigger - this indicates HANDLING state is wedged.\n658\t            // The watchdog runs in a separate thread and can detect when the worker\n659\t            // is stuck even if the Tokio runtime becomes unresponsive.\n660\t            if self.watchdog_triggered.load(Ordering::Relaxed)\n661\t                && self.state == WorkerState::Handling\n662\t            {\n663\t                tracing::error!(\"watchdog detected HANDLING state hang, forcing recovery\");\n664\t                // Emit critical timeout event.\n665\t                let bead_id = self.current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.id.clone());\n666\t                let _ = self\n667\t                    .telemetry\n668\t                    .emit_try_lock(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n669\t                        bead_id: bead_id.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| BeadId::from(\"unknown\")),\n670\t                        outcome: \"unknown\".to_string(),\n671\t                        operation: \"watchdog\".to_string(),\n672\t                        error: format!(\n673\t                            \"HANDLING state exceeded {}s timeout\",\n674\t                            HANDLING_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT_SECS\n675\t                        ),\n676\t                    });\n677\t                // Attempt best-effort release if we have a bead.\n678\t                if let Some(ref bead) = self.current_bead {\n679\t                    let bead_id = bead.id.clone();\n680\t                    tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead_id, \"best-effort bead release due to watchdog timeout\");\n681\t                    let _ =\n682\t                        tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(30), self.store.release(&bead_id))\n683\t                            .await;\n684\t                }\n685\t                // Clear the watchdog trigger and force transition to LOGGING.\n686\t                self.watchdog_triggered.store(false, Ordering::Release);\n687\t                self.handling_state_entered_at = None;\n688\t                // Force transition to LOGGING to recover.\n689\t                self.set_state(WorkerState::Logging)?;\n690\t            }\n691\t\n692\t            match self.state {\n693\t                WorkerState::Selecting => self.do_select().await?,\n694\t                WorkerState::Claiming => self.do_claim().await?,\n695\t                WorkerState::Retrying => self.do_retry().await?,\n696\t                WorkerState::Building => self.do_build().await?,\n697\t                WorkerState::Dispatching => self.do_dispatch().await?,\n698\t                WorkerState::Executing => self.do_execute().await?,\n699\t                WorkerState::Handling => self.do_handle().await?,\n700\t                WorkerState::Logging => self.do_log()?,\n701\t                WorkerState::Exhausted => {\n702\t                    let next = self.handle_exhausted().await?;\n703\t                    match next {\n704\t                        WorkerState::Selecting => continue,\n705\t                        terminal => return Ok(terminal),\n706\t                    }\n707\t                }\n708\t                WorkerState::Stopped => {\n709\t                    return self.stop(\"normal shutdown\").await;\n710\t                }\n711\t                WorkerState::Errored => {\n712\t                    let err = self\n713\t                        .last_error\n714\t                        .take()\n715\t                        .unwrap_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"unknown error\"));\n716\t                    let msg = format!(\"{err}\");\n717\t                    self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerErrored {\n718\t                        error_type: \"worker_scoped\".to_string(),\n719\t                        error_message: msg.clone(),\n720\t                        beads_processed: self.beads_processed,\n721\t                    })?;\n722\t\n723\t                    // Emit WorkerStopped before exiting so telemetry shows a clean shutdown.\n724\t                    // This ensures operators can distinguish \"exited with error\" from\n725\t                    // \"killed by external agent\" (e.g., SIGKILL, OOM).\n726\t                    let uptime = self.boot_time.map(|t| t.elapsed().as_secs()).unwrap_or(0);\n727\t                    let _ = self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerStopped {\n728\t                        reason: format!(\"error: {msg}\"),\n729\t                        beads_processed: self.beads_processed,\n730\t                        uptime_secs: uptime,\n731\t                    });\n732\t\n733\t                    // Best-effort stop heartbeat on error.\n734\t                    self.health.stop();\n735\t                    // Best-effort deregister on error.\n736\t                    if let Err(e) = self.registry.deregister(&self.qualified_id()) {\n737\t                        tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"failed to deregister from worker registry on error\");\n738\t                    }\n739\t                    self.telemetry.shutdown().await;\n740\t                    return Err(err);\n741\t                }\n742\t                WorkerState::Booting => {\n743\t                    bail!(\"boot() should have transitioned past Booting state\");\n744\t                }\n745\t            }\n746\t        }\n747\t    }\n748\t\n749\t    // ── Boot ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n750\t\n751\t    /// Validate configuration and initialize the worker.\n752\t    ///\n753\t    /// Each step is instrumented with `init.step.started`/`init.step.completed`\n754\t    /// events so that hangs are visible in the telemetry log. Boot duration is\n755\t    /// capped at 60 seconds — if exceeded, the worker self-aborts with a\n756\t    /// `worker.boot.timeout` event and exits with a non-zero code.\n757\t    fn boot(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n758\t        self.boot_time = Some(Instant::now());\n759\t        const BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 60;\n760\t\n761\t        // Step: Config validation\n762\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepStarted {\n763\t            step: \"config_validation\".to_string(),\n764\t        })?;\n765\t        let step_start = Instant::now();\n766\t        let errors = ConfigLoader::validate(&self.config);\n767\t        if !errors.is_empty() {\n768\t            let msg = errors\n769\t                .iter()\n770\t                .map(|e| e.to_string())\n771\t                .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n772\t                .join(\"; \");\n773\t            bail!(\"config validation failed: {msg}\");\n774\t        }\n775\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepCompleted {\n776\t            step: \"config_validation\".to_string(),\n777\t            duration_ms: step_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,\n778\t        })?;\n779\t\n780\t        // Check boot timeout before each step\n781\t        self.check_boot_timeout(BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECS)?;\n782\t\n783\t        // Step: Registry registration\n784\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepStarted {\n785\t            step: \"registry_registration\".to_string(),\n786\t        })?;\n787\t        let step_start = Instant::now();\n788\t        let qualified_id = format!(\"{}-{}\", self.config.agent.default, self.worker_name);\n789\t        let entry = WorkerEntry {\n790\t            id: qualified_id.clone(),\n791\t            pid: std::process::id(),\n792\t            workspace: self.config.workspace.default.clone(),\n793\t            agent: self.config.agent.default.clone(),\n794\t            model: None,\n795\t            provider: self.resolve_provider(),\n796\t            started_at: chrono::Utc::now(),\n797\t            beads_processed: 0,\n798\t        };\n799\t        if let Err(e) = self.registry.register(entry) {\n800\t            tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"failed to register in worker registry\");\n801\t        }\n802\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepCompleted {\n803\t            step: \"registry_registration\".to_string(),\n804\t            duration_ms: step_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,\n805\t        })?;\n806\t\n807\t        // Check boot timeout before each step\n808\t        self.check_boot_timeout(BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECS)?;\n809\t\n810\t        // Step: Heartbeat emitter start\n811\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepStarted {\n812\t            step: \"heartbeat_emitter\".to_string(),\n813\t        })?;\n814\t        let step_start = Instant::now();\n815\t        if let Err(e) = self.health.start_emitter() {\n816\t            tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"failed to start heartbeat emitter\");\n817\t        }\n818\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepCompleted {\n819\t            step: \"heartbeat_emitter\".to_string(),\n820\t            duration_ms: step_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,\n821\t        })?;\n822\t\n823\t        // Emit worker started event — boot complete\n824\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerStarted {\n825\t            worker_name: self.worker_name.clone(),\n826\t            version: env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\").to_string(),\n827\t        })?;\n828\t\n829\t        self.set_state(WorkerState::Selecting)?;\n830\t\n831\t        tracing::info!(\n832\t            worker = %self.worker_name,\n833\t            strands = ?self.strands.strand_names(),\n834\t            \"worker booted\"\n835\t        );\n836\t\n837\t        Ok(())\n838\t    }\n839\t\n840\t    /// Check if boot has exceeded the timeout and abort if so.\n841\t    ///\n842\t    /// Emits `worker.boot.timeout` and exits the process with a non-zero\n843\t    /// code. This is a last-resort measure when an init step hangs.\n844\t    fn check_boot_timeout(&self, timeout_secs: u64) -> Result<()> {\n845\t        if let Some(boot_start) = self.boot_time {\n846\t            let elapsed = boot_start.elapsed();\n847\t            if elapsed.as_secs() > timeout_secs {\n848\t                let elapsed_ms = elapsed.as_millis() as u64;\n849\t                // Emit the timeout event before aborting\n850\t                let _ = self\n851\t                    .telemetry\n852\t                    .emit(EventKind::WorkerBootTimeout { elapsed_ms });\n853\t                tracing::error!(\n854\t                    elapsed_ms,\n855\t                    \"boot timeout exceeded {}s — aborting\",\n856\t                    timeout_secs\n857\t                );\n858\t                // Flush telemetry before exit\n859\t                std::mem::forget(self.telemetry.clone());\n860\t                // Exit with a distinct code to indicate boot timeout\n861\t                std::process::exit(71); // EX_OSERR + custom offset\n862\t            }\n863\t        }\n864\t        Ok(())\n865\t    }\n866\t\n867\t    // ── Signal handling ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n868\t\n869\t    /// Install SIGINT, SIGTERM, and SIGHUP handlers that set the shutdown flag.\n870\t    ///\n871\t    /// SIGHUP is handled because when the parent bash dies (e.g., tmux session\n872\t    /// killed, external reaper), the child process receives SIGHUP by default.\n873\t    /// Without a handler, the process terminates immediately without emitting\n874\t    /// worker.stopped telemetry or flushing the telemetry buffer.\n875\t    ///\n876\t    /// Uses synchronous signal handlers via libc/signal-hook to ensure signals\n877\t    /// are caught immediately, even if the tokio runtime hasn't polled async\n878\t    /// signal tasks yet. This prevents silent process termination when signals\n879\t    /// arrive early (e.g., SIGHUP from parent bash death during worker startup).\n880\t    fn install_signal_handlers(&self) {\n881\t        // Store a global reference to the shutdown flag for signal handlers.\n882\t        // We use a leak to ensure the reference lives for the entire program duration.\n883\t        let shutdown_ptr = Arc::into_raw(self.shutdown.clone()) as usize;\n884\t        set_global_shutdown_flag(shutdown_ptr);\n885\t\n886\t        // Register atexit handler to emit worker.stopped telemetry on unexpected termination.\n887\t        // This provides diagnostic information when the worker is killed by an external\n888\t        // process (e.g., capacity governor, OOM killer, SIGKILL).\n889\t        let start_time = self.boot_time.unwrap_or_else(Instant::now);\n890\t        register_atexit_handler(\n891\t            self.worker_name.clone(),\n892\t            self.beads_processed,\n893\t            start_time,\n894\t            format!(\"{:?}\", self.state),\n895\t            None, // log_file_path - not available during boot\n896\t        );\n897\t\n898\t        #[cfg(unix)]\n899\t        {\n900\t            // Install synchronous signal handlers using libc.\n901\t            // These handlers are called immediately when the signal is received,\n902\t            // before the tokio runtime has a chance to process any async tasks.\n903\t            unsafe {\n904\t                install_unix_signal_handlers();\n905\t            }\n906\t        }\n907\t\n908\t        #[cfg(not(unix))]\n909\t        {\n910\t            // On non-Unix platforms, use tokio's ctrl_c handler.\n911\t            let shutdown_int = self.shutdown.clone();\n912\t            tokio::spawn(async move {\n913\t                if tokio::signal::ctrl_c().await.is_ok() {\n914\t                    tracing::info!(\"received SIGINT, initiating graceful shutdown\");\n915\t                    shutdown_int.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n916\t                }\n917\t            });\n918\t        }\n919\t    }\n920\t\n921\t    // ── State handlers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n922\t\n923\t    /// SELECTING: run strand waterfall to find a candidate bead.\n924\t    async fn do_select(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n925\t        // Clear per-cycle state.\n926\t        // Preserve race-lost exclusions with TTL and beads that lost a race in the current cycle.\n927\t        // NOTE: Do NOT reset retry_count or consecutive_race_lost here — they must\n928\t        // accumulate across cycles to prevent infinite race-lost loops (see needle-aad8).\n929\t        self.race_lost_this_cycle.clear();\n930\t        self.current_bead = None;\n931\t        self.current_strand = None;\n932\t\n933\t        // Restore home store if it was swapped for a remote workspace.\n934\t        self.restore_home_store();\n935\t\n936\t        // Update heartbeat with home workspace (not current_workspace which\n937\t        // might be stale). This ensures heartbeat reports correctly even if\n938\t        // restore_home_store() was a no-op (stores already equal).\n939\t        self.health.update_state(\n940\t            &WorkerState::Selecting,\n941\t            None,\n942\t            Some(self.config.workspace.default.as_path()),\n943\t        );\n944\t\n945\t        // Try atomic claim_auto first (server-selected bead in one transaction).\n946\t        // This eliminates the race condition where two workers both see the same\n947\t        // bead in ready() and race to claim it.\n948\t        let strand = \"auto\";\n949\t        let claim = self.claimer.claim_auto(&self.qualified_id(), strand).await;\n950\t\n951\t        match claim {\n952\t            Ok(ClaimResult::Claimed(bead)) => {\n953\t                tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"atomically claimed bead via claim_auto\");\n954\t                self.current_bead = Some(bead);\n955\t                self.current_strand = Some(strand.to_string());\n956\t                self.consecutive_race_lost = 0;\n957\t                self.set_state(WorkerState::Building)?;\n958\t                return Ok(());\n959\t            }\n960\t            Ok(ClaimResult::NotClaimable { reason }) => {\n961\t                tracing::debug!(\n962\t                    reason,\n963\t                    \"claim_auto returned no beads, falling back to strand waterfall\"\n964\t                );\n965\t                // Fall through to strand waterfall\n966\t            }\n967\t            Err(e) => {\n968\t                tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"claim_auto failed, falling back to strand waterfall\");\n969\t                // Fall through to strand waterfall\n970\t            }\n971\t            Ok(other) => {\n972\t                tracing::warn!(\n973\t                    ?other,\n974\t                    \"claim_auto returned unexpected result, falling back to strand waterfall\"\n975\t                );\n976\t                // Fall through to strand waterfall\n977\t            }\n978\t        }\n979\t\n980\t        // Fallback: run strand waterfall to find a candidate bead.\n981\t        let candidate\n989\t            .strand_evaluations\n990\t            .iter()\n991\t            .map(|e| (e.strand_name.clone(), e.result.clone(), e.duration_ms))\n992\t            .collect();\n993\t\n994\t        match candidate.bead {\n995\t            Some((bead, strand_name)) => {\n996\t                tracing::debug!(bead_id = %bead.id, strand = %strand_name, \"candidate found\");\n997\t\n998\t                // If the bead is from a remote workspace (found by Explore),\n999\t                // swap the active store so claim/show/release operate on the\n1000\t      let mut sample: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n381\t\n382\t        for ev in events.iter().rev() {\n383\t            if ev.event_type == \"bead.claim.race_lost\" {\n384\t                if let state_transition events and extract state sequences.\n409\t        let mut state_transitions: Vec<(DateTime<Utc>, String, String)> = Vec::new();\n410\t\n411\t        for ev in events.iter().rev()           .map(|(_, _, to)| to.clone())\n435\t            .collect();\n436\t\n437\t        // Count unique states in the window.\n438\t        let unique_states: HashSet<_> = });\n458\t            }\n459\t        }\n460\t\n461\t        None\n462\t    }\n463\t\n464\t    /// Detect log runaway: excessive file growth without completion events.\n465\t    fn
